- 模型 DeepSeek：`/root/autodl-tmp/models/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct/deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6___7b-instruct`

### 3. 📊 当前状态
**刚做完**：收到 exception_only_seed123 的 cron 完成回调（进程正常退出，ANALYZING 状态）。已派 Worker `worker_analysis_exception_only_seed123_20260515T180236` 去 SSH 读取 eval_results.json 并更新 registry。**该 Worker 尚未回调**——下一个 session 需要等它完成或手动检查。

**Registry 关键状态**：
- `exception_only_seed123`: ANALYZING → 等 Worker 读结果转 DONE
- `exception_only_seed456`: DONE/success, accuracy=99.83%（与 seed42 完全一致）
- `vota_seed456`: PREPARING（Director 已降优先级，因 VOTA 在 DeepSeek 退化到 84.5%）
- `deepseek_exception_only_seed123`: PREPARING（跨模型 seed robustness）
- 4 个 QUEUED 实验（vota_seed42/v2, no_trace_seed42/v2）均为早期 killed 残留，可忽略

**等待中**：
- Worker 回调：`worker_analysis_exception_only_seed123_20260515T180236`（读 eval 结果）
- Worker 回调：`worker_code_fix_evaluate_truncation_20260515T165903`（修 evaluate.py truncation，已跑 1h+，turn 22）
- 3 个 fig2_calibration Worker 在跑但多为重复/卡住——建议清理

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- `worker_analysis_exception_only_seed123` 回调 → 读 metrics，确认 accuracy ≈ 99.83% → 更新 report.md → 向 Director 汇报 seed robustness 3/3 完成（seed42=99.83%, seed456=99.83%, seed123=?）
- seed123 accuracy ≈ 99.83% → plan_008a seed robustness 完成，exception_only 跨 3 seed 稳定，强化 Claim 1
- seed123 accuracy < 99% → 触发 red-section，产出 Framing 调整提案
- `worker_code_fix_evaluate_truncation` 回调 → 用修复后代码重跑 DeepSeek full_trace（cuda:0），修复 D018 invalidation
- GPU 空闲后 → 优先安排：(1) DeepSeek full_trace fix 重跑 (2) deepseek_exception_only_seed123 跨模型 seed robustness (3) plan_005 DebugBench 第二任务（如时间允许）
- fig2_calibration Worker 清理 → 检查哪个产出了有效图表，取消其余重复 Worker
- Paper Agent 启动条件：framing 更新完成 + seed robustness 确认后立即启动（ROADMAP 说"今天启动"）

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**核心结论**：Exception events 是 verdict prediction 的 universal diagnostic signal。跨模型 gap = 0pp（Qwen + DeepSeek epoch1 均 99.83%）。VOTA 的价值是 fault indicator isolation，不是 trace compression。

**决策链关键节点**：
- D019: 跨模型验证完成，exception signal universal
- D018: DeepSeek full_trace collapsed 因 train-eval truncation mismatch（非 chat template 问题），需 v5 代码重跑
- D017: DeepSeek exception_only epoch1=99.83%=Qwen，但 epoch3 降到 99.33%（过拟合）
- D013: ae_only 99.92% 不构成 data leakage（Reviewer 已裁定 fallback locals 无信息量）
- D020: ae_only 进入 Claim 3 层级表 co-Tier 1，但 headline 仍用 exception_only

**易错点**：
- DeepSeek exception_only 最佳结果在 epoch1 (99.83%)，不是 final epoch3 (99.33%)——汇报时注意区分
- eval_loss 与 accuracy 解耦：eval_loss 上升不一定意味着 accuracy 下降（但 DeepSeek 此处恰好同时降了）
- vota_seed456 被 Director 降优先级——不要主动去跑，除非 Director 重新要求
- ARR May 25 DDL 还剩 10 天，Paper Agent 是今天最高优先级启动项
- Worker 池 6 上限，fig2 系列占了太多 slot——新 session 先清理再铺新任务

```json
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

_source line 40 · 2026-05-15T21:06:44.196Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01PYMDwjiF7hsQtDNvaKFSBV`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

```json
      "content": "---\nname: resource-saturation\ndescription: \"当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。\"\nalways: false\nroles:\n  - director\n  - agent\n---\n\n# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）\n\n## A. GPU 分配率检查\n\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\n\n任何\"分配了但没跑\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\"暂无可铺实验\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\n\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\n\n## B. GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",
```

```text
- Worker pool: 1 (only CPU task)

1. The experiments finished between the registry read and the resource check
2. The cron hasn't caught up yet
3. The processes died


Since the crons were just created 63 seconds ago and haven't reached their polling interval yet, the actual GPU status is still uncertain. The resource tool might be showing stale data because the cron hasn't confirmed the experiments are actually running on hardware. I should check the server directly to see what's really happening, though I need to be careful about how I do that as an Agent. since both crons were skipped due to being too recently created).

Actually, looking more carefully at the reconcile output: both crons exist but were skipped because they were created only 63 seconds ago. The experiments are in RUNNING status in the registry. The resource tool shows 0 actually running because it cross-references with nvidia-smi or something similar, which it can't do remotely.

I think the resource tool just checks registry RUNNING experiments. But it shows 0 running... Let me re-read the registry output.

Looking at registry again:
- `deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v3`: status = "RUNNING" 
- `deepseek_exception_only_seed456`: status = "RUNNING"

But the resource tool says "running_experiments": []. That's strange. Maybe the resource tool checks differently.

OK, I think there may be a mismatch. Either the experiments actually completed and the crons haven't caught up yet, or the resource tool is using a different check. Given the timing—the first one started around 18:40 UTC and has been running for about 2.5 hours (typical training duration), while the second started at 20:19 UTC and is only an hour in—I should spawn a Worker to SSH and verify the actual status of both experiments. The reconcile output showing both crons skipped due to being created 63 seconds ago suggests they were just registered and will poll on the next interval, but the resource tool showing them as idle is concerning.

I need to immediately check what's happening by spawning a Worker to verify GPU status and experiment logs, then report to the Director. I should also address the L1 violations: both GPUs potentially idle and the Worker pool at 1 when it should be at least 2. Let me spawn workers to check GPU and experiment status, and handle the worker pool issue, then prepare the Director report covering the Qwen exception_only results and the DeepSeek experiments.

Now I'm seeing that _trace_v3 is still running but needs verification, VOTA seed456_v2 completed at 99.08%, and both GPUs are showing as idle. I'll spawn an SSH worker to check server status and rely on the already-running paper-data-tables worker to bring the pool to 2, which satisfies the minimum requirement. While waiting for the GPU experiments, I can parallelize paper work on Claim 2 or 3 sections since Claim 1 is paused according to the roadmap, or run a literature scan.

I'll spawn the SSH worker to verify GPU status and both running experiments, start the Paper Agent on Claim 2/3 sections, and report to the Director with the current known state and what's pending verification.
```

````json
{
  "task_type": "ops:gpu-status-check",
  "project": "trace-verdict-study",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：检查 westd-11235 上两个 RUNNING 实验的实际状态\n\n### 背景\nRegistry 显示两个实验 RUNNING，但资源检查工具报告 0 张 GPU 在跑。需要 SSH 到服务器确认实际情况。\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 服务器：westd-11235，账号 root\n- Python：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n- 工作目录：`/root/trace-verdict-study`\n\n### 检查步骤\n\n1. **GPU 进程检查**：\n```bash\nnvidia-smi --query-compute-apps=pid,gpu_uuid,used_memory --format=csv,noheader 2>/dev/null || nvidia-smi\n```\n\n2. **tmux session 检查**（训练通过 tmux 提交）：\n```bash\ntmux list-sessions 2>/dev/null\n```\n\n3. **实验 1: deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v3**（约 18:40 UTC 启动，~2.5h 前，可能已完成）\n```bash\n# 检查进程是否还在\nps aux | grep deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v3 | grep -v grep\n# 检查 eval 结果是否已生成\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v3/final/eval_results.json 2>/dev/null\ncat /root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v3/final/eval_results.json 2>/dev/null\n# 检查训练日志最后几行\ntail -20 /root/runs/trace-verdict-study/deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v3.log 2>/dev/null\n```\n\n4. **实验 2: deepseek_exception_only_seed456**（约 20:19 UTC 启动，~1h 前，可能还在跑）\n```bash\n# 检查进程\nps aux | grep deepseek_exception_only_seed456 | grep -v grep\n# 检查 checkpoint 目录\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/deepseek_exception_only_seed456/ 2>/dev/null\n# 检查 eval 结果\ncat /root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/deepseek_exception_only_seed456/final/eval_results.json 2>/dev/null\n# 日志\ntail -20 /root/runs/trace-verdict-study/deepseek_exception_only_seed456.log 2>/dev/null\n```\n\n5. **磁盘空间**（以防磁盘满导致实验中断）：\n```bash\ndf -h / /root/autodl-tmp/\n```\n\n### 产出要求\n用 `update_registry` 更新实验状态：\n- 如果实验已完成且有 eval_results.json：读取 metrics，更新 registry 为 DONE（conclusion=success 或 negative，附 metrics）\n- 如果实验还在跑：报告当前进度（第几步、loss 等）\n- 如果实验进程已死但没有结果：标记 DONE/failed，记录原因\n\n每个实验都必须给出明确结论，不要留模糊状态。\n",

  ]
````

```json
{
  "task_type": "research:paper-claim2-prep",
  "project": "trace-verdict-study",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：整理 Claim 2 (Information Density > Gradient Dilution) 的论文素材\n\n### 背景\ntrace-verdict-study 项目正在准备 EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25 投稿（DDL 10 天后）。Paper Agent 已启动，Claim 1 因 DeepSeek seed 不鲁棒正在修订。**Claim 2 和 Claim 3 可以先行**。\n\n### Claim 2 核心论点\n\"Information density > gradient dilution\"：full_trace 性能受限的主要原因是 gradient dilution（loss 在长 trace token 上稀释），而非 trace 信息不足。Label-only loss 部分修复了 gradient dilution（+4.4pp），但 VOTA 的 decision-relevant compression 才是根本解法。\n\n### 支撑实验数据（从 registry 读取确认）\n用 `read_registry` 读取以下实验的 metrics，整理成论文表格格式：\n\n| Condition | Accuracy | 关键对比 |\n|-----------|----------|----------|\n| full_trace_seed42_v5 | 81.76% | raw trace baseline |\n| full_trace_label_only_seed42 | 86.22% | label-only loss, +4.4pp vs full_trace |\n| vota_seed42_v5 | 99.41% | VOTA, +17.6pp vs full_trace |\n| no_trace_seed42_v5 | 75.13% | no trace baseline |\n\n### 产出要求\n1. 读 `read_project_file(file=\"paper_framing.md\")` 了解 Claim 2 的具体 framing\n2. 读 registry 确认上述实验 metrics 准确无误\n3. 写一份 claim2_evidence_brief.md 到项目 artifacts 目录，包含：\n   - Claim 2 statement\n   - 支撑数据表格（含 CI、统计检验）\n   - 关键论证逻辑（gradient dilution → label-only partial fix → VOTA full fix）\n   - 需要的图表建议（哪些图能可视化这个 claim）\n   - 潜在 reviewer 攻击点 + 预防措施\n4. 用 `register_artifact` 注册产出文件\n",
  "tool_groups": [
    "knowledge",
    "registry"
  ]
}
```
